## 應用輻射地形扁率 (影像分析工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 合成孔徑雷達工具集

**english**　Apply Radiometric Terrain Flattening

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/apply-radiometric-terrain-flattening.html

**id**　fda6afdcaf885d0959-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/apply-radiometric-terrain-flattening.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Apply Radiometric Terrain Flattening，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入雷達資料 [in_radar_data]、輸出雷達資料 [out_radar_data]、DEM 網格 [in_dem_raster]、應用大地水準面校正 （可選） [geoid]、極化波段 （可選） [polarization_bands]、校準類型 （可選） [calibration_type]、輸出散射區域 （可選） [out_scattering_area]、輸出幾何畸變 （可選） [out_geometric_distortion]、輸出幾何畸變掩膜 （可選） [out_geometric_distortion_mask]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/image-analyst/apply-radiometric-terrain-flattening.html#parameters

## 應用輻射地形扁率 (影像分析工具) · 英文版工具簽章與參數順序

**english**　Apply Radiometric Terrain Flattening

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 影像分析工具箱
- 合成孔徑雷達工具集

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/apply-radiometric-terrain-flattening.html

**id**　fda6afdcaf885d0959-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/image-analyst/apply-radiometric-terrain-flattening.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/image-analyst/apply-radiometric-terrain-flattening.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_radar_data", "label_zh": "輸入雷達資料", "label_en": "Input Radar Data", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Raster Dataset; Raster Layer", "description_en": "The input radar data. The data that will have radiometric terrain flattening applied. The data must be radiometrically calibrated to beta nought."}, {"parameter": "out_radar_data", "label_zh": "輸出雷達資料", "label_en": "Output Radar Data", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Raster Dataset", "description_en": "The radiometrically terrain-flattened radar data."}, {"parameter": "in_dem_raster", "label_zh": "DEM 網格", "label_en": "DEM Raster", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Mosaic Layer; Raster Layer", "description_en": "The input DEM. The DEM that will be used to estimate the local illuminated area and the local incidence angle."}, {"parameter": "geoid", "label_zh": "應用大地水準面校正 （可選）", "label_en": "Apply geoid correction (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the vertical reference system of the input DEM will be transformed to ellipsoidal height. Most elevation datasets are referenced to sea level orthometric height, so a correction is required in these cases to convert to ellipsoidal height. Checked—A geoid correction will be made to convert orthometric height to ellipsoidal height (based on EGM96 geoid). This is the default. Unchecked—No geoid correction will be made. Use this option only if the DEM is provided in ellipsoidal height."}, {"parameter": "polarization_bands", "label_zh": "極化波段 （可選）", "label_en": "Polarization Bands (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The polarization bands that will be radiometrically terrain flattened. The first band is selected by default. Select all polarization bands if the output will be with the SAR Analysis tools."}, {"parameter": "calibration_type", "label_zh": "校準類型 （可選）", "label_en": "Calibration Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether the output will be terrain flattened using sigma nought or gamma nought. Gamma nought—The beta nought backscatter will be corrected using an accurate computation of an area using a DEM. This is the default. Sigma nought—The beta nought backscatter will be corrected using the unit area of a plane that is locally tangent to the DEM."}, {"parameter": "out_scattering_area", "label_zh": "輸出散射區域 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Scattering Area (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Dataset", "description_en": "The scattering area radar dataset."}, {"parameter": "out_geometric_distortion", "label_zh": "輸出幾何畸變 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Geometric Distortion (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Dataset", "description_en": "The 4-band geometric distortion radar dataset. The first band is the terrain slope, the second band is look angle, the third band is the foreshortening ratio, and the fourth band is the local incidence angle."}, {"parameter": "out_geometric_distortion_mask", "label_zh": "輸出幾何畸變掩膜 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Geometric Distortion Mask (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Dataset", "description_en": "The 1-band geometric distortion mask radar dataset. The pixels are classified using six unique values, one for each distortion type: Undetermined —Value of 0 Foreshortening —Value of 1 Lengthening —Value of 2 Shadow —Value of 3 Layover —Value of 4 Layover and shadow —Value of 5"}]

**signatures**　
- ApplyRadiometricTerrainFlattening(in_radar_data, out_radar_data, in_dem_raster, {geoid}, {polarization_bands}, {calibration_type}, {out_scattering_area}, {out_geometric_distortion}, {out_geometric_distortion_mask})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_radar_data、out_radar_data、in_dem_raster、geoid、polarization_bands、calibration_type、out_scattering_area、out_geometric_distortion、out_geometric_distortion_mask。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。
